<div class="cell-wrapper layout-widget-wrapper">
<span class="hs_cos_wrapper hs_cos_wrapper_widget hs_cos_wrapper_type_custom_widget" data-hs-cos-general-type="widget" data-hs-cos-type="custom_widget" id="hs_cos_wrapper_module_1462332634233933" style=""><script>
    $(document).ready(function(){
    // Populate hs_context input
    var hs_context = {
        "hutk": "", 
        "ipAddress": "185.217.117.209", 
        "pageUrl": "https://www.pocketmath.com", 
        "pageName": "Pocketmath | Home",
        "redirectUrl": "https://www.pocketmath.com"
    };
    $('input[name="hs_context"]').val(JSON.stringify(hs_context));

    if ($('.select2').length > 0) {
        // Convert select2
        $('.select2').select2();
    }

    // Convert country into select2 with country options
    if ($('select[name="country"]').length > 0) {
        var countryInput = $('select[name="country"]');
        var selectedCountry;
        // Add hidden input
        countryInput.parent().append('<input type="hidden" name="core_market" value="" />');
        countryInput.select2({
            placeholder: "Select a Country",
            data: countries.map(function(item){
                return {id: item.country, text: item.country};
            })
        });
        countryInput.select2("val", "");
        countryInput.on("change",function(evt){
           selectedCountry = countries.find(function(c){ return c.country === evt.target.value });
           $('input[name="core_market"]').val(selectedCountry.region);
        });
    }
    
    var requiredInputs = $('[required]').toArray();
    
    function validateForm(evt) {
        // Disabling the submit button
        var button = $('[type="submit"]');
        var buttonText = button.text();
        button.prop('disabled', true);
        if (button.data('disable-with')) {
            button.text(button.data('disable-with'))
        }
        
        // Reset the errors
        $('.form-group.has-error .help-block').remove();
        $('.form-group.has-error').removeClass('has-error');
    
        // Validate form
        var form = $('form');
        var errors = [];
        var el, name, msg;
        for (var input of requiredInputs){
            el = $(input)
            name = el.prop('placeholder') || el.data('placeholder');
            if( el.val() === "" || el.val() === null ){
                msg = name + ' is required';
                errors.push(msg);
                el.parents('.form-group').addClass('has-error');
                el.parent().append('<span class="help-block">' + msg + '</span>');
            }
        }
        
        if (errors.length === 0) {
            return; // submit the form!
        } else {
            button.prop('disabled', false).text(buttonText);
            evt.preventDefault();
        }
    };
    
    $('form').submit(validateForm);
});
</script></span></div><!--end layout-widget-wrapper -->
